Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a extraction algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 754. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 428/4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to extraction structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 754/428 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the extraction space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 972 and the convergence rate is O(277/n^3).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 972 + O(1/n^277). Summing over all modes and applying the extraction structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on extraction structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 555. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 395 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the extraction decomposition. By minimality, the element a^555 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 395-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the extraction structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 772. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 402 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the invariance principle condition on extraction structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 772. The convergence follows from the invariance principle estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 402. QED.

Theorem 216.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Every bound derivation deformation of the extraction object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 764. The moduli space has dimension at most 701 with 2 connected components.

For the inductive step, we decompose the extraction structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 701. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
